Use when the user says "audit my env vars", "update .env.example", "what env vars does this app need", "it works on my machine but not in prod", "why is this variable undefined", or "document the environment variables". ([vivekbhange-hub/ship-check-skill](https://github.com/vivekbhange-hub/ship-check-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shipcheck-env))
- **shipcheck-preflight**: The 60-second pre-flight for anything about to ship: runs the secrets scan, the env audit, the Dockerfile lint and the CI audit in order, merges the findings into a one-page go or no-go report (blockers, should-fix, advisories) and refuses to say "ship it" while any high-severity finding is open. Use when the user says "pre-flight check", "am I ready to deploy", "check before I push", "run all the ship checks", "is this safe to release", or "go or no-go". ([vivekbhange-hub/ship-check-skill](https://github.com/vivekbhange-hub/ship-check-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shipcheck-preflight))
- **shipcheck-secrets**: Scans a project, and optionally its recent git history, for leaked secrets (AWS, GitHub, Slack, Stripe and Google keys, private keys, JWTs, database URLs with passwords, generic password and token assignments, high-entropy strings) with masked output, then runs a rotation-first response: assume the secret is burned, revoke it first, clean history only afterwards and only if the user wants. Use when the user says "scan for secrets", "did I commit an API key", "check this before I push", "I pushed a token by accident", "is my .env safe", "rotate this key", or "find leaked credentials". ([vivekbhange-hub/ship-check-skill](https://github.com/vivekbhange-hub/ship-check-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shipcheck-secrets))
- **shipcheck-submission**: Prepare ShipCheck's hackathon submission: production end-to-end test table (Phase 8), the README structure with the containment table and the Vultr services section (Phase 10), the 2-3 minute demo video script, the 8 slides, the cover image, the lablab.ai submission texts and tags, and the final pre-submit checklist (Phase 11, submit by 18:00 PKT Sun 8 Nov). Use when writing the README, planning or scripting the video, making slides, filling the lablab.ai form or running final release checks. ([shah541-g/ShipCheck](https://github.com/shah541-g/ShipCheck/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/shipcheck-submission) · 3 copies)
- **shipcheck-vultr-deploy**: Provision and deploy ShipCheck on Vultr: firewall group, Ubuntu 24.04 VM, deploy user and SSH hardening, swap, Docker, gVisor runsc, Serverless Inference key and model choice, Object Storage bucket, Caddy on sslip.io, the production docker-compose (caddy/web/api/egress-proxy, internal sandbox network with the proxy at 172.30.0.10), the production .env, updates, troubleshooting and teardown. Use for any VM, infrastructure, deployment or production-debugging task on ShipCheck (build-steps Phases 1, 7, 12 and Troubleshooting). ([shah541-g/ShipCheck](https://github.com/shah541-g/ShipCheck/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shipcheck-vultr-deploy) · 3 copies)
